A grown-up-sized long-sleeved belted one-piece zipper leisure suit in corduroy, with wide full-length legs, sewn entirely from real fabric as a wearable version of the outfit of a data courier. On the upper body it is a padded jacket with LED stripes, sewn from felt and velour, with belts, patches and buttons attached as real pieces. The lower half is made as trousers in faux fur, joined to the top as one piece. An attached hood at the neck is sewn as goggles in felt. A fabric version of data case is stitched onto the suit. The garment is assembled from cut panels of fabric joined with visible seams, topstitching and piping, and it hangs as one coherent piece with every part in the outfit's own cloth. The uniform fills the whole straight-sided garment shape edge to edge, the same width at chest, waist and hem. A full data courier costume, head to toe in a single suit, with a padded jacket with LED stripes, trousers on the legs, goggles (as the hood), boots (as the feet), a data case sewn or attached to the suit. Nothing of the outfit is missing and the legs of the suit show the lower garment. Novelty embellishments, glitter-flecked tulle layered at the hem, layered ruffle cape-like shoulders and terry-loop textured stripes. The embellishments are sewn on as real raised fabric, so the suit has depth, texture and sparkle from every angle.

An attached hood at the neck is sewn as a captain's hat in denim. A fabric version of harpoon is stitched onto the suit. The suit is built from layered fabric pieces sewn together, colour-blocked in the outfit's colours, with finished edges and neat stitching throughout. The outfit is stretched to the garment's own straight-sided shape and fills it completely, so it suits any body size. It is a complete costume of kraken sailor in one piece, shown full length from collar to ankles, with a tentacled coat, a captain's hat (as the hood), boots (as the feet), a harpoon sewn or attached to the suit. The upper half carries the top and the leg tubes carry the lower garment, so the whole outfit reads at once. Novelty embellishments, metallic foil-print accents that catch the light, chunky pom-pom trim along the hood edge and cuffs and hand-stitched patchwork appliqué in bright colours. Everything is chosen to be fun to look at and tactile to feel, bumpy, fluffy, shiny and soft in turn.

An attached hood at the neck is sewn as a lion head in denim. It is tailored like a real garment, with proper seams, darts, hems and stitching lines, entirely in the outfit's own colours and cloths. The outfit is laid out on the garment's own rectangle shape, fully covering it from side seam to side seam. It is a complete costume of lion dancer in one piece, shown full length from collar to ankles, with a furry lion costume, a lion head (as the hood). The upper half carries the top and the leg tubes carry the lower garment, so the whole outfit reads at once. Novelty embellishments, dangling fabric tassels on the zip pull and hood, corduroy ridged panels and rhinestone buttons down the front. It is a novelty costume garment, made to be bright, playful, over-the-top and a pleasure to touch.

The suit is built from layered fabric pieces sewn together, colour-blocked in the outfit's colours, with finished edges and neat stitching throughout. The uniform fills the whole straight-sided garment shape edge to edge, the same width at chest, waist and hem. A full dragon rider costume, head to toe in a single suit, with a hardened leather flight jacket with scale pads, trousers on the legs, a goggled helm (as the hood), boots (as the feet), a saddle strap sewn or attached to the suit. Nothing of the outfit is missing and the legs of the suit show the lower garment. It is a novelty costume garment, made to be bright, playful, over-the-top and a pleasure to touch.

An attached hood at the neck is sewn as a sack mask in chenille. A fabric version of pitchfork is stitched onto the suit. It is tailored like a real garment, with proper seams, darts, hems and stitching lines, entirely in the outfit's own colours and cloths. The uniform fills the whole straight-sided garment shape edge to edge, the same width at chest, waist and hem. A full scarecrow king costume, head to toe in a single suit, with a straw-stuffed jacket, a sack mask (as the hood), boots (as the feet), a pitchfork sewn or attached to the suit. Nothing of the outfit is missing and the legs of the suit show the lower garment. Novelty embellishments, glitter-flecked tulle layered at the hem, braided cord drawstrings with knotted ends and padded 3D patch pockets. Everything is chosen to be fun to look at and tactile to feel, bumpy, fluffy, shiny and soft in turn.
An adult-sized long-sleeved racing-stripe one-piece zipper leisure suit in boucle, with straight full-length legs, sewn entirely from real fabric as a wearable version of the outfit of a harvest king. On the upper body it is a straw cape, sewn from vinyl-look fabric and denim, with belts, patches and buttons attached as real pieces. An attached hood at the neck is sewn as a crown of wheat in vinyl-look fabric. The shirt's sleeves are tailored to the outfit's cut, wide or flared where the outfit's sleeves flare, ending at the same slant or curve, with the print filling the whole sleeve shape. A fabric version of sickle is stitched onto the suit. The suit is built from layered fabric pieces sewn together, colour-blocked in the outfit's colours, with finished edges and neat stitching throughout. The outfit is laid out on the garment's own rectangle shape, fully covering it from side seam to side seam. The suit is the whole harvest king outfit, not just a top, with a straw cape, a crown of wheat (as the hood), a sickle sewn or attached to the suit. It is shown in full, from the neckline down to the ankle cuffs. Novelty embellishments, faux-fur cuffs and collar, embroidered patches and crests with stitched borders and dangling fabric tassels on the zip pull and hood. Everything is chosen to be fun to look at and tactile to feel, bumpy, fluffy, shiny and soft in turn.
An adult-sized long-sleeved racing-stripe one-piece zipper leisure suit in chenille, with straight full-length legs, sewn entirely from real fabric as a wearable version of the outfit of a giant. The upper body is made as a huge hide tunic, in sequin fabric with organza trims, every collar, lapel, strap and pocket a separate sewn piece. A fabric version of club is stitched onto the suit. The garment is assembled from cut panels of fabric joined with visible seams, topstitching and piping, and it hangs as one coherent piece with every part in the outfit's own cloth. The outfit is stretched to the garment's own straight-sided shape and fills it completely, so it suits any body size. It is a complete costume of giant in one piece, shown full length from collar to ankles, with a huge hide tunic, a club sewn or attached to the suit. The upper half carries the top and the leg tubes carry the lower garment, so the whole outfit reads at once. Novelty embellishments, contrast panels in clashing, playful colours, chunky pom-pom trim along the hood edge and cuffs and rhinestone buttons down the front. It is a novelty costume garment, made to be bright, playful, over-the-top and a pleasure to touch.

Every part is real fabric, panels cut, stitched together and finished with seam binding, with the outfit's details made as sewn pieces. The uniform fills the whole straight-sided garment shape edge to edge, the same width at chest, waist and hem. It is a complete costume of idol singer on stage in one piece, shown full length from collar to ankles, with a frilled stage dress, ribbons (as the hood), boots (as the feet), a microphone sewn or attached to the suit. The upper half carries the top and the leg tubes carry the lower garment, so the whole outfit reads at once. Novelty embellishments, dangling fabric tassels on the zip pull and hood, metallic foil-print accents that catch the light and chunky pom-pom trim along the hood edge and cuffs. Everything is chosen to be fun to look at and tactile to feel, bumpy, fluffy, shiny and soft in turn.
